                          <h1 class="shade-05" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Troubleshooting
                          </h1>
                          <p class="shade-10" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Common issues and how to fix them.
                          </p>
                          <h2 class="shade-05" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Can&#39;t log in
                          </h2>
                          <p class="shade-10" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Make sure you&#39;re using the email address tied to your
Slite workspace. Reset your password from the login page
if needed.
                          </p>
                          <h2 class="shade-05" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Docs not loading
                          </h2>
                          <p class="shade-10" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Try a hard refresh (Cmd+Shift+R) or clear your browser
cache. If the issue persists, try a different browser.
                          </p>
                          <h2 class="shade-05" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Search not returning results
                          </h2>
                          <p class="shade-10" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Re-index your workspace from Settings → Advanced →
Reindex. Results update within a few minutes.
                          </p>
                          <h2 class="shade-05" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Notifications not arriving
                          </h2>
                          <p class="shade-10" style="opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)">
                            Check that notifications are enabled in Settings →
Notifications and that Slite isn&#39;t blocked by your
browser or OS.
                          </p>

                <h2 class="font-size-2 font-family-title font-weight-medium line-height-normal website-text-title margin-bottom-32 shade-00" style="max-width:500px;margin-right:auto;margin-left:auto;text-align:center">
                  Slite rewrites outdated documentation on autopilot
                </h2>
                <p class="font-size-4 line-height-large website-text-body-lg margin-bottom-32 shade-00" style="max-width:550px;margin-right:auto;margin-left:auto;text-align:center">
                  Slite Agent watches Slack, Linear, GitHub and your codebase, spots outdated facts in your documentation, and drafts the update for you to approve in one click.
                </p>
                <div class="margin-bottom-60">

              <h2 class="font-size-2 font-family-title font-weight-medium line-height-normal website-text-title margin-bottom-32 shade-00" style="max-width:500px;margin-right:auto;margin-left:auto;text-align:center">
                Slite rewrites outdated documentation on autopilot
              </h2>
              <p class="font-size-4 line-height-large website-text-body-lg margin-bottom-32 shade-00" style="max-width:550px;margin-right:auto;margin-left:auto;text-align:center">

            <div style="border-radius:32px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;text-align:start;padding:48px 48px 0;min-width:240px;flex:1;overflow:hidden" class="background-moon-dust-lighter css-1xcho5r">
              <h3 class="font-size-3 font-weight-semibold line-height-title website-text-smallTitle margin-bottom-16 shade-00 font-family-title" style="max-width:280px;font-weight:500;font-size:28px">
                Ask from anywhere
              </h3>
              <p class="font-size-6 line-height-large website-text-body margin-bottom-32 shade-00" style="max-width:280px">
                Slite, Slack, the Chrome extension, or any AI tool over MCP. The same answer wherever your people and agents ask.
              </p>
              <img src="/assets/static/ask-from-anywhere.B0kxoHR6.svg" alt="" aria-hidden="true" draggable="false" style="display:block;width:calc(100% + 96px);max-width:none;height:auto;aspect-ratio:387 / 312;margin:auto -48px 0" />
            </div>
            <div style="border-radius:32px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;text-align:start;padding:48px 48px 0;min-width:240px;flex:1;overflow:hidden" class="background-moon-dust-lighter css-1xcho5r">
              <h3 class="font-size-3 font-weight-semibold line-height-title website-text-smallTitle margin-bottom-16 shade-00 font-family-title" style="max-width:280px;font-weight:500;font-size:28px">
                Permission-aware
              </h3>
              <p class="font-size-6 line-height-large website-text-body margin-bottom-32 shade-00" style="max-width:280px">
                Yes, even for third-party agents. Every query inherits the user&#39;s permissions.
              </p>
              <img src="/assets/static/permission-aware.LSHoGYVM.svg" alt="" aria-hidden="true" draggable="false" style="display:block;width:calc(100% + 96px);max-width:none;height:auto;aspect-ratio:387 / 301;margin:auto -48px 0" />
            </div>
            <div style="border-radius:32px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;text-align:start;padding:48px 48px 0;min-width:240px;flex:1;overflow:hidden" class="background-moon-dust-lighter css-1xcho5r">
              <h3 class="font-size-3 font-weight-semibold line-height-title website-text-smallTitle margin-bottom-16 shade-00 font-family-title" style="max-width:280px;font-weight:500;font-size:28px">
                One connection to your company context
              </h3>
              <p class="font-size-6 line-height-large website-text-body margin-bottom-32 shade-00" style="max-width:280px">
                Connect Slite Agent once instead of wiring an MCP for every tool. Less setup, less cost, one set of permissions.
              </p>
              <img src="/assets/static/one-connection.CR6aXx4W.svg" alt="" aria-hidden="true" draggable="false" style="display:block;width:calc(100% + 96px);max-width:none;height:auto;aspect-ratio:387 / 270;margin:auto -48px 0" />
            </div>

            <div class="css-1kmcf2h">
              <div style="flex:1;text-align:left;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:32px;padding-right:32px">
                <p class="font-size-4 line-height-large website-text-body-lg shade-00" style="text-align:start">
                  The same engine that powers the Agent is exposed via MCP. Your verified knowledge becomes the source of truth for Claude, ChatGPT, Cursor, and anything else your company plugs in.
                </p>

                <div class="css-1aby622">
                  <p class="font-size-3 margin-bottom-8 line-height-normal font-size-4-small line-height-large">
                    “
                    <!-- -->
                    We use Slite MCP on Claude a lot for documentation. Our team also uses Slite&#39;s AI search directly to find docs across Slack, Drive, and more.
                    <!-- -->
                    ”
                  </p>
                  <div>
                    <span class="font-size-6 line-height-large website-text-body shade-20">                    Alexis Dupont
</span>
                    <span class="font-size-6 line-height-large website-text-body shade-20">                    ,
                    <!-- -->
                    Director of AI Transformation at Agorapulse
</span>
                  </div>
